   242	    try {
   243	      return await fn();
   244	    } catch (err) {
   245	      const msg = String(/** @type {any} */ (err)?.message ?? err);
   246	      if (/already exists/i.test(msg)) throw err; // a stale branch is a park, not a heal
   247	      await selfHealWorktree(root, { seat, branch: `baton/wt-${seat}/base` }, io);
   248	      return fn();
   249	    }
   250	  };
   251	
   252	  for (let i = state.phaseIndex; i < subtasks.length; i += 1) {
   253	    const st = subtasks[i];
   254	    const seat = i % 2 === 0 ? 'a' : 'b';
   255	    const other = seat === 'a' ? 'b' : 'a';
   256	    const branch = `baton/wt-${seat}/subtask-${st.id}`;
   257	    const gate = `subtask-${st.id}-review`;
   258	
   259	    // A crash between merge and PHASE_ADVANCE leaves the branch fully merged:
   260	    // recognize it and advance instead of a misleading empty-branch park (H7).
   261	    // Recognition requires BOTH a supervisor-owned merge receipt AND the
   262	    // branch tip being an ancestor of main (I1): is-ancestor alone
   263	    // false-positives on a stale empty branch parked at main's old tip (a
   264	    // writer that crashed before its first commit), and a receipt alone can
   265	    // outlive a rewound main. Ancestor-without-receipt parks as stale;
   266	    // receipt-without-ancestor re-runs the subtask.
   267	    let isAncestor = false;
   268	    try {
   269	      await io.execFile('git', ['merge-base', '--is-ancestor', branch, 'main'], { cwd: root });
   270	      isAncestor = true;
   271	    } catch {
   272	      // Not an ancestor (or the branch does not exist yet) — the normal path.
   273	    }
   274	    if (isAncestor) {
   275	      if (mergeReceipts().some((r) => r?.subtaskId === st.id)) {
   276	        io.stdout.write(`baton pipeline run: subtask '${st.id}' is already merged into main (receipt + ancestor) — advancing\n`);
   277	        await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.PHASE_ADVANCE });
   278	        continue;
   279	      }
   280	      return park(`subtask '${st.id}' has a stale branch '${branch}' sitting at main's tip with no merge receipt — a writer likely crashed before committing; delete the branch and resume`);
   281	    }
   282	
   283	    const writerAssignment = resolveOne(`worker-${seat}`);
   284	    const reviewerModel = resolveOne(`worker-${other}`);
   285	    const mergerModel = resolveOne('merger');
   286	    if (!writerAssignment || !reviewerModel || !mergerModel) {
   287	      return park(`no eligible assignment for subtask '${st.id}' (worker-${seat}/worker-${other}/merger must all resolve)`);
   288	    }
   289	
   290	    // Start the subtask branch in the writer's seat (self-healing a raw-deleted
   291	    // seat), then verify the seat. A pre-existing (stale) branch parks.
   292	    let mainSha;
   293	    try {
   294	      const alreadyOnBranch =
   295	        String((await withSeatHealing(seat, () => io.execFile('git', ['rev-parse', '--abbrev-ref', 'HEAD'], { cwd: seats[seat] }))).stdout).trim() === branch;
   296	      if (!alreadyOnBranch) await withSeatHealing(seat, () => io.execFile('git', ['checkout', '-b', branch], { cwd: seats[seat] }));
   297	      const pre = await preflightWorktree(root, { seat, branch }, io);
   298	      if (pre.ok !== true) return park(`preflight refused subtask '${st.id}': ${pre.refusal}`);
   299	      mainSha = (await io.execFile('git', ['rev-parse', 'main'], { cwd: root })).stdout.trim();
   300	    } catch (err) {
   301	      return park(`seat preparation failed for subtask '${st.id}': ${String(/** @type {any} */ (err)?.message ?? err).split('\n')[0]}`);
   302	    }
   303	
   304	    let findings = '';
   305	    let merged = false;
   306	    let failoverAttempt = 0;
   307	    /** @type {Array<{platform: string, model: string}>} */
   308	    let subtaskAvoidEntries = [];
   309	    /** @type {any | null} */
   310	    let forcedWriter = null;
   311	    while (!merged) {
   312	      if ((state.iterations?.[gate] ?? 0) >= cap) {
   313	        await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.GATE_ITERATION, gate, verdict: 'BLOCKED' });
   314	        atomicWriteText(
   315	          io.fs,
   316	          `${p.dir}/ESCALATION.md`,
   317	          `# Pipeline escalation\n\nGate '${gate}' exhausted its ${cap}-iteration cap.\nLast findings:\n\n${findings}\n`,
   318	        );
   319	        io.stderr.write(`baton pipeline run: gate '${gate}' hit the ${cap}-iteration cap — escalated (see ${p.dir}/ESCALATION.md)\n`);
   320	        return EXIT_ESCALATED;
   321	      }
   322	
   323	      // Writer (write-capable, its own seat).
   324	      const writerAsg = forcedWriter ?? { ...writerAssignment, role: `worker-${seat}` };
   325	      forcedWriter = null;
   326	      const writerPrompt = [
   327	        `You are the writer (worker-${seat}) for subtask '${st.id}': ${st.title}.`,
   328	        `Goal: ${spec.goal}. Work ONLY on branch ${branch} in your worktree; commit your work there.`,
   329	        findings ? `The previous attempt was BLOCKED — address every finding:\n${findings}` : '',
   330	        "When your work is committed and self-checked, End with exactly:\nVERDICT: APPROVED | APPROVED_WITH_NOTES | BLOCKED\nFINDINGS: numbered findings, or 'none'",
   331	      ]
   332	        .filter(Boolean)
   333	        .join('\n\n');
   334	      const writerResult = await spawn(writerAsg, writerPrompt, seats[seat], `${st.id}-writer`);
   335	
   336	      // The writer's result is CONSUMED, never discarded (Gate-2 fold G3):
   337	      // classify its log first — a limit/model death routes through failover,
   338	      // a BLOCKED self-check retries the writer, and only a passing writer
   339	      // hands off to review.
   340	      const writerLog = writerResult.logPath && io.fs.existsSync(writerResult.logPath) ? io.fs.readFileSync(writerResult.logPath, 'utf8') : '';
   341	      const writerCls = classify({ text: writerLog, exitCode: writerResult.exitCode ?? 0, platform: writerAsg.platform, table }).class;
   342	      if (writerCls !== 'ok') {
   343	        failoverAttempt += 1;
   344	        // Failover is BOUNDED: at most one relaunch per configured chain entry
   345	        // for this seat — beyond that the subtask parks instead of ping-ponging
   346	        // across platforms forever (H2).
   347	        const chainLen = Array.isArray(config?.roles?.[`worker-${seat}`]) ? config.roles[`worker-${seat}`].length : 1;
   348	        if (failoverAttempt > chainLen) {
   349	          return park(`subtask '${st.id}' exhausted its failover budget (${chainLen} chain entr${chainLen === 1 ? 'y' : 'ies'}, ${failoverAttempt} deaths) — parked instead of looping`);
   350	        }
   351	        const decision = await runFailover({
   352	          root,
   353	          io,
   354	          config,
   355	          role: `worker-${seat}`,
   356	          assignment: writerAsg,
   357	          transcript: writerLog,
   358	          exitCode: writerResult.exitCode ?? 1,
   359	          sessionHint: `loop-${state.runId}`,
   360	          probes: null,
   361	          avoid: [],
   362	          avoidEntries: subtaskAvoidEntries,
   363	          attempt: failoverAttempt,
   364	          loopState: { runId: state.runId, phaseIndex: state.phaseIndex, iterations: state.iterations, status: state.status },
   365	        });
   366	        if (decision.action === 'park') return park(`writer failover parked subtask '${st.id}': ${decision.reason}`);
   367	        if (decision.action === 'relaunch') {
   368	          // Carry entry-level avoidance across relaunches — a rejected model
   369	          // stays rejected for this subtask (H2).
   370	          if (Array.isArray(decision.avoidEntries)) subtaskAvoidEntries = decision.avoidEntries;
   371	          forcedWriter = { ...decision.assignment, role: `worker-${seat}` };
   372	        }
   373	        continue; // relaunch or retry the writer — never review a failed attempt
   374	      }
   375	      if (writerResult.verdict !== 'APPROVED' && writerResult.verdict !== 'APPROVED_WITH_NOTES') {
   376	        findings = String(writerResult.findings ?? '');
   377	        const after = await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.GATE_ITERATION, gate, verdict: 'BLOCKED' });
   378	        if (after.status === LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED) {
   379	          atomicWriteText(io.fs, `${p.dir}/ESCALATION.md`, `# Pipeline escalation\n\nGate '${gate}' exhausted its ${cap}-iteration cap.\nLast findings:\n\n${findings}\n`);
   380	          return EXIT_ESCALATED;
   381	        }
   382	        continue; // the writer retries; no reviewer sees a failed attempt
   383	      }
   384	
   385	      const post = await postflightWorktree(root, { seat, branch, mainSha }, io);
   386	      if (post.ok !== true) return park(`postflight refused subtask '${st.id}': ${post.refusal}`);
   387	
   388	      // An empty branch never merges (Gate-2 fold G3): the writer must have
   389	      // actually committed work ahead of main.
   390	      const ahead = String((await io.execFile('git', ['log', `main..${branch}`, '--format=%H'], { cwd: root })).stdout).trim();
   391	      if (ahead.length === 0) return park(`subtask '${st.id}' produced an EMPTY branch (no commits ahead of main) — nothing to review or merge`);
   392	
   393	      // Reviewer-seat preflight (Gate-2 fold G5): the reviewing seat must be
   394	      // listed and clean before a reviewer spawns into it.
   395	      try {
   396	        const otherBranch = String((await withSeatHealing(other, () => io.execFile('git', ['rev-parse', '--abbrev-ref', 'HEAD'], { cwd: seats[other] }))).stdout).trim();
   397	        const reviewerPre = await preflightWorktree(root, { seat: other, branch: otherBranch }, io);
   398	        if (reviewerPre.ok !== true) return park(`reviewer-seat preflight refused subtask '${st.id}': ${reviewerPre.refusal}`);
   399	      } catch (err) {
   400	        return park(`reviewer-seat preparation failed for subtask '${st.id}': ${String(/** @type {any} */ (err)?.message ?? err).split('\n')[0]}`);
   401	      }
   402	
   403	      // Reviewer (read-only, the OTHER seat's model and cwd, subtask-reviewer role).
   404	      const reviewPrompt = [
   405	        `You are the subtask-reviewer for subtask '${st.id}': ${st.title}.`,
   406	        `Review the change on ${branch} against main from the ${other} seat (fresh context). Run: git diff main..${branch} (and git log main..${branch}) to see exactly what changed — branch refs are shared across worktrees.`,
   407	        "End with exactly:\nVERDICT: APPROVED | APPROVED_WITH_NOTES | BLOCKED\nFINDINGS: numbered findings, or 'none'",
   408	      ].join('\n\n');
   409	      const review = await spawn({ ...reviewerModel, role: 'subtask-reviewer' }, reviewPrompt, seats[other], `${st.id}-review`);
   410	      if (review.verdict !== 'APPROVED' && review.verdict !== 'APPROVED_WITH_NOTES') {
   411	        findings = String(review.findings ?? '');
   412	        const after = await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.GATE_ITERATION, gate, verdict: 'BLOCKED' });
   413	        if (after.status === LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED) {
   414	          atomicWriteText(io.fs, `${p.dir}/ESCALATION.md`, `# Pipeline escalation\n\nGate '${gate}' exhausted its ${cap}-iteration cap.\nLast findings:\n\n${findings}\n`);
   415	          return EXIT_ESCALATED;
   416	        }
   417	        continue;
   418	      }
   419	
   420	      // Merger (read-only child — it re-checks; the SUPERVISOR merges).
   421	      const mergerPrompt = [
   422	        `You are the merger for subtask '${st.id}'. Adversarially re-check ${branch} against main before it merges.`,
   423	        "End with exactly:\nVERDICT: APPROVED | APPROVED_WITH_NOTES | BLOCKED\nFINDINGS: numbered findings, or 'none'",
   424	      ].join('\n\n');
   425	      const mergerVerdict = await spawn({ ...mergerModel, role: 'merger' }, mergerPrompt, root, `${st.id}-merge-check`);
   426	      if (mergerVerdict.verdict !== 'APPROVED' && mergerVerdict.verdict !== 'APPROVED_WITH_NOTES') {
   427	        findings = String(mergerVerdict.findings ?? '');
   428	        const after = await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.GATE_ITERATION, gate, verdict: 'BLOCKED' });
   429	        if (after.status === LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED) {
   430	          atomicWriteText(io.fs, `${p.dir}/ESCALATION.md`, `# Pipeline escalation\n\nGate '${gate}' exhausted its ${cap}-iteration cap.\nLast findings:\n\n${findings}\n`);
   431	          return EXIT_ESCALATED;
   432	        }
   433	        continue;
   434	      }
   435	
   436	      const merge = await mergeSubtask(root, { branch }, io);
   437	      if (merge.ok !== true) return park(`merge of '${branch}' refused: ${merge.reason}`);
   438	      // The receipt lands BEFORE the phase-advance journal write (I1): a
   439	      // crash between the two resumes into a receipt-backed advance.
   440	      appendEntry(io.fs, mergesPath, { subtaskId: st.id, branch, mergedAt: io.now() });
   441	      merged = true;
   442	    }
   443	
   444	    await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.PHASE_ADVANCE });
   445	  }
   446	
   447	  if (flags.json) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: true, data: { status: state.status, subtasks: subtasks.length } });
   448	  else io.stdout.write(`baton pipeline run: done — ${subtasks.length} subtask(s) merged\n`);
   449	  return state.status === LOOP_STATUS.DONE ? 0 : state.status === LOOP_STATUS.PARKED ? EXIT_PARKED : 0;
   450	}

 succeeded in 0ms:
   120	 * Shared by `loop run` and `pipeline run`.
   121	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io @param {string} cmdLabel
   122	 * @returns {Promise<{ok: true, release: () => void} | {ok: false, code: number}>}
   123	 */
   124	export async function acquireSupervisorLock(root, io, cmdLabel) {
   125	  const p = loopPaths(root);
   126	  const lockPath = `${p.dir}/supervisor.lock`;
   127	  const childrenPath = `${p.dir}/children.ndjson`;
   128	  ensureDir(io.fs, p.dir);
   129	  const payload = JSON.stringify({ host: io.host, pid: io.pid, startTime: io.startTime, runId: `sup-${io.pid}` });
   130	
   131	  const tryExclusive = () => {
   132	    // Fast-path an already-present lock (fakes without O_EXCL semantics rely
   133	    // on this); the wx flag below is the REAL exclusivity on a POSIX fs — a
   134	    // competitor that lands between the check and the write throws EEXIST
   135	    // instead of being clobbered.
   136	    if (io.fs.existsSync(lockPath)) return false;
   137	    try {
   138	      io.fs.writeFileSync(lockPath, payload, { flag: 'wx' });
   139	      return true;
   140	    } catch (err) {
   141	      if (/** @type {any} */ (err)?.code === 'EEXIST') return false;
   142	      throw err;
   143	    }
   144	  };
   145	
   146	  for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= 2; attempt += 1) {
   147	    if (tryExclusive()) {
   148	      // A fresh acquisition owns a fresh child registry: prior invocations'
   149	      // start/retire records are truncated so a stale retire can never mask a
   150	      // later in-flight child that reuses the same childId (I2).
   151	      try {
   152	        if (io.fs.existsSync(childrenPath)) io.fs.unlinkSync(childrenPath);
   153	      } catch {
   154	        // Best effort.
   155	      }
   156	      return {
   157	        ok: true,
   158	        release: () => {
   159	          try {
   160	            if (io.fs.existsSync(lockPath)) io.fs.unlinkSync(lockPath);
   161	          } catch {
   162	            // Best effort — a stale lock is recoverable via the dead path.
   163	          }
   164	        },
   165	      };
   166	    }
   167	    const existing = safeReadJson(io.fs, lockPath);
   168	    const other = existing.ok ? existing.value : null;
   169	    // Liveness verifies the (pid, startTime) PAIR — a recycled pid without the
   170	    // recorded start time is a dead owner, not a live supervisor (I5).
   171	    const alive = other && typeof other.pid === 'number' && typeof io.processAlive === 'function' ? io.processAlive(other.pid, other.startTime) : true;
   172	    const sameProcess = other && other.pid === io.pid && other.startTime === io.startTime;
   173	    if (!sameProcess && (!other || other.host !== io.host || alive)) {
   174	      io.stderr.write(`baton ${cmdLabel}: another supervisor (pid ${other?.pid ?? 'unknown'} on ${other?.host ?? 'unknown'}, run ${other?.runId ?? '?'}) holds the run lock — refusing a second supervisor\n`);
   175	      return { ok: false, code: 1 };
   176	    }
   177	    // Provably dead (or our own stale lock): reap its recorded in-flight
   178	    // child groups BEFORE anything else runs, then reclaim.
   179	    if (io.fs.existsSync(childrenPath)) {
   180	      const lines = String(io.fs.readFileSync(childrenPath, 'utf8')).split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim() !== '');
   181	      // Match retires to starts IN ORDER per childId — a resumed invocation
   182	      // reuses childIds, and a prior run's retire must never mask the newer
   183	      // in-flight start of the same id (I2). Whatever start remains unmatched
   184	      // is genuinely in-flight.
   185	      /** @type {Map<string, any[]>} */
   186	      const inflight = new Map();
   187	      for (const line of lines) {
   188	        try {
   189	          const rec = JSON.parse(line);
   190	          if (typeof rec?.endedAt === 'string') inflight.get(rec.childId)?.shift();
   191	          else if (typeof rec?.pgid === 'number') {
   192	            if (!inflight.has(rec.childId)) inflight.set(rec.childId, []);
   193	            inflight.get(rec.childId)?.push(rec);
   194	          }
   195	        } catch {
   196	          // A torn record is unreapable — skip it.
   197	        }
   198	      }
   199	      // Kill ONLY genuinely in-flight children (start with no matching
   200	      // retire): a retired child's pgid may have been recycled by an
   201	      // unrelated live process — killing it would be a pid-reuse casualty (H3).
   202	      for (const recs of inflight.values()) {
   203	        for (const rec of recs) {
   204	          if (typeof io.processAlive === 'function' && io.processAlive(rec.pgid)) {
   205	            if (typeof io.processKill === 'function') io.processKill(-rec.pgid, 'SIGKILL');
   206	          }
   207	        }
   208	      }
   209	      try {
   210	        io.fs.unlinkSync(childrenPath);
   211	      } catch {
   212	        // Best effort.
   213	      }
   214	    }
   215	    io.stderr.write(`baton ${cmdLabel}: reclaimed the run lock from a provably-dead supervisor (pid ${other?.pid})\n`);
   216	    try {
   217	      io.fs.unlinkSync(lockPath);
   218	    } catch {
   219	      // Already gone.
   220	    }
   221	  }
   222	  io.stderr.write(`baton ${cmdLabel}: could not acquire the run lock after reclaiming — another supervisor keeps winning\n`);
   223	  return { ok: false, code: 1 };
   224	}
   225	
   226	/**
   227	 * The supervisor state machine: drives the spec's phases through headless
   228	 * role children, records every transition in the journal-replayable loop
   229	 * state, enforces the 5-cap, pauses at the smoke gate, and hands limit
   230	 * deaths to the failover transaction. Children run via the injectable seam
   231	 * `io.superviseChild ?? superviseChild`.
   232	 * @param {Record<string, string | boolean>} flags @param {any} io
   233	 * @returns {Promise<number>}
   234	 */
   235	async function runLoop(flags, io) {
   236	  const root = resolveRoot(io, flags);
   237	  const specPath = `${root}/loop.json`;
   238	  const p = loopPaths(root);
   239	  const lockPath = `${p.dir}/supervisor.lock`;
   240	
   241	  // Preconditions — before any lock or spawn.
   242	  const rawSpec = safeReadJson(io.fs, specPath);
   243	  if (!rawSpec.ok) {
   244	    return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', `no loop.json at ${specPath} — scaffold one with: baton loop init "<goal>"`);
   245	  }
   246	  const { config } = loadConfig(root, io);
   247	  if (!config) return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', `no readable baton.config.json at ${root} — the role matrix is required`);
   248	  const validated = validateLoopSpec(rawSpec.value, config);
   249	  if (validated.ok !== true) {
   250	    return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', `loop.json is invalid:\n  - ${validated.errors.join('\n  - ')}`);
   251	  }
   252	  const spec = validated.spec;
   253	
   254	  // Supervisor run lock — atomic exclusive acquisition; provably-dead owners
   255	  // are reclaimed with their recorded child groups reaped first (B8 + G4).
   256	  const lock = await acquireSupervisorLock(root, io, 'loop run');
   257	  if (lock.ok !== true) return lock.code;
   258	
   259	  // Load or initialize the run state — stamped with {flavor, specDigest} so
   260	  // cross-flavor, changed-spec, or pre-stamp resumes refuse instead of
   261	  // misaligning (H5 + I3). These refusals release the lock explicitly — an
   262	  // early return here must not leave supervisor.lock behind (I4).
   263	  const specDigest = dedupeKey(spec.phases);
   264	  let { state } = await loadLoopState(root, io);
   265	  if (state === null) {
   266	    state = { ...initLoopState(spec, io), flavor: 'loop', specDigest };
   267	    await writeLoopState(root, state, io);
   268	  } else {
   269	    if (typeof state.flavor === 'string' && state.flavor !== 'loop') {
   270	      lock.release();
   271	      return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', `the persisted run state is flavor '${state.flavor}' — a ${state.flavor} run cannot be resumed as a loop (flavor mismatch); archive .handoff/loop or finish the ${state.flavor} run first`);
   272	    }
   273	    if (typeof state.flavor !== 'string' || typeof state.specDigest !== 'string') {
   274	      lock.release();
   275	      return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', 'the persisted run state is missing its {flavor, specDigest} stamp (pre-stamp legacy state) — resuming it could misalign the run; archive .handoff/loop to start fresh');
   276	    }
   277	    if (state.specDigest !== specDigest) {
   278	      lock.release();
   279	      return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', 'loop.json phases changed since this run started (spec digest mismatch) — resuming would misalign the phase position; archive .handoff/loop to start fresh');
   280	    }
   281	  }
   282	  const runId = state.runId;
   283	  // The lock payload's runId now correlates with the run it guards (H8).
   284	  io.fs.writeFileSync(`${p.dir}/supervisor.lock`, JSON.stringify({ host: io.host, pid: io.pid, startTime: io.startTime, runId }));
   285	
   286	  /** Apply one event, journal it, persist the snapshot — one transition. */
   287	  const transition = async (/** @type {any} */ ev) => {
   288	    const next = applyLoopEvent(state, ev);
   289	    const seq = await appendLoopEvent(root, ev, io);
   290	    state = { ...next, journalSeq: seq };
   291	    await writeLoopState(root, state, io);
   292	    return state;
   293	  };
   294	
   295	  const table = loadSignatures({ builtinPath: BUILTIN_SIGNATURES }, io);
   296	  const runner = io.superviseChild ?? superviseChild;
   297	  const sessionHint = `loop-${runId}`;
   298	  /** @type {string[]} */
   299	  let avoid = [];
   300	  /** @type {Array<{platform: string, model: string}>} */
   301	  let avoidEntries = [];
   302	  let childSeq = 0;
   303	
   304	  try {
   305	    // `loop resume` — a PARK is resumable; an escalation is operator-only.
   306	    if (flags.__resume === true) {
   307	      if (state.status === LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED) {
   308	        io.stderr.write(`baton loop resume: the run is ESCALATED (gate ${state.escalation?.gate}) — escalation is an operator decision; resolve the findings and start a fresh gate instead\n`);
   309	        return EXIT_ESCALATED;
   310	      }
   311	      if (state.status === LOOP_STATUS.PARKED) {
   312	        await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.RESUME });
   313	        io.stdout.write('baton loop resume: the parked run is running again\n');
   314	      }
   315	    }
   316	
   317	    // --approve-smoke: verify the presented token against RECOMPUTED inputs;
   318	    // drift parks (the human approved a state that no longer exists).
   319	    if (typeof flags['approve-smoke'] === 'string') {
   320	      if (state.status !== LOOP_STATUS.AWAITING_SMOKE_APPROVAL) {
   321	        return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', `--approve-smoke only applies while the run awaits smoke approval (status: ${state.status})`);
   322	      }
   323	      const record = safeReadJson(io.fs, `${p.dir}/smoke-approval.json`);
   324	      if (!record.ok) return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', 'no smoke-approval.json found — run the loop to the smoke gate first');
   325	      const git = await gitSnapshot({ execFile: io.execFile, cwd: root, fs: io.fs });
   326	      const currentInputs = {
   327	        stateDigest: dedupeKey(io.fs.readFileSync(p.state, 'utf8')),
   328	        smokeCmd: String(spec.smoke?.cmd ?? ''),
   329	        smokeOutputDigest: String(record.value?.inputs?.smokeOutputDigest ?? ''),
   330	        gitHead: gitHeadOf(git),
   331	        gitContentDigest: gitContentOf(git),
   332	        childAssignment: String(record.value?.inputs?.childAssignment ?? ''),
   333	      };
   334	      const verify = verifySmokeToken(String(flags['approve-smoke']), currentInputs);
   335	      if (verify.ok !== true) {
   336	        const drifted = 'driftedInputs' in verify ? verify.driftedInputs.join(', ') : 'unknown';
   337	        await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.PARK, reason: `smoke approval token is stale — drifted inputs: ${drifted}; re-run the smoke gate` });
   338	        io.stderr.write(`baton loop run: smoke approval REFUSED — inputs drifted since the token was issued (${drifted}); the run is parked for a fresh smoke pass\n`);
   339	        return EXIT_PARKED;
   340	      }
   341	      await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.SMOKE_APPROVE, token: flags['approve-smoke'], verified: true });
   342	      io.stdout.write('baton loop run: smoke approval verified — continuing the run\n');
   343	    }
   344	
   345	    if (state.status === LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED) {
   346	      io.stderr.write(`baton loop run: the run is escalated (gate ${state.escalation?.gate}) — see ${p.dir}/ESCALATION.md\n`);
   347	      return EXIT_ESCALATED;
   348	    }
   349	    if (state.status === LOOP_STATUS.PARKED) {
   350	      io.stderr.write(`baton loop run: the run is parked — ${state.parkReason ?? 'no reason recorded'}\n`);
   351	      return EXIT_PARKED;
   352	    }
   353	    if (state.status === LOOP_STATUS.AWAITING_SMOKE_APPROVAL) {
   354	      io.stdout.write(`baton loop run: awaiting smoke approval — resume with: baton loop run --approve-smoke <token from ${p.dir}/smoke-approval.json>\n`);
   355	      return 0;
   356	    }
   357	
   358	    // ---- The phase loop. ---------------------------------------------------
   359	    while (state.status === LOOP_STATUS.RUNNING && state.phaseIndex < state.phaseCount) {
   360	      const phase = spec.phases[state.phaseIndex];
   361	      const cap = spec.budgets.iterationCap;
   362	      let findings = '';
   363	      let failoverAttempt = 0;
   364	      /** @type {any | null} */
   365	      let forcedAssignment = null;
   366	      /** @type {string | null} */
   367	      let forcedPrompt = null;
   368	      let phaseDone = false;
   369	
   370	      while (!phaseDone) {
   371	        // The cap gates the SPAWN: at the cap the reducer escalates and a
   372	        // 6th child never starts.
   373	        if ((state.iterations?.[phase.id] ?? 0) >= cap) {
   374	          await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.GATE_ITERATION, gate: phase.id, verdict: 'BLOCKED' });
   375	          atomicWriteText(
   376	            io.fs,
   377	            `${p.dir}/ESCALATION.md`,
   378	            `# Loop escalation\n\nGate '${phase.id}' exhausted its ${cap}-iteration cap on run ${runId}.\nLast findings:\n\n${findings}\n\nEscalation is operator-only: review and resolve the findings with the operator before any new gate attempt.\n`,
   379	          );
   380	          io.stderr.write(`baton loop run: gate '${phase.id}' hit the ${cap}-iteration cap — escalated (see ${p.dir}/ESCALATION.md)\n`);
   381	          return EXIT_ESCALATED;
   382	        }
   383	        if (childSeq >= spec.budgets.maxChildrenPerPhase * spec.phases.length) {
   384	          await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.PARK, reason: 'total child budget exhausted' });
   385	          return EXIT_PARKED;
   386	        }
   387	
   388	        const resolved =
   389	          forcedAssignment ??
   390	          (() => {
   391	            const r = resolveRoles({ config, to: 'claude-code', avoid, avoidEntries, probes: null }).assignments[phase.role];
   392	            return r && r.mode !== 'unavailable' ? r : null;
   393	          })();
   394	        if (!resolved) {
   395	          await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.PARK, reason: `no eligible platform/model for role '${phase.role}'` });
   396	          io.stderr.write(`baton loop run: no eligible assignment for role '${phase.role}' — parked\n`);
   397	          return EXIT_PARKED;
   398	        }
   399	        const assignment = { ...resolved, role: phase.role };
   400	        const prompt =
   401	          forcedPrompt ??
   402	          [
   403	            `You are the ${phase.role} for loop run ${runId}, phase '${phase.id}'.`,
   404	            `Goal: ${spec.goal}`,
   405	            spec.constraints.length > 0 ? `Constraints:\n${spec.constraints.map((/** @type {string} */ c) => `- ${c}`).join('\n')}` : '',
   406	            findings ? `The previous attempt was BLOCKED with these findings — address every one:\n${findings}` : '',
   407	            "End with exactly:\nVERDICT: APPROVED | APPROVED_WITH_NOTES | BLOCKED\nFINDINGS: numbered findings, or 'none'",
   408	          ]
   409	            .filter(Boolean)
   410	            .join('\n\n');
   411	        forcedAssignment = null;
   412	        forcedPrompt = null;
   413	
   414	        childSeq += 1;
   415	        const childId = `${String(childSeq).padStart(3, '0')}-${phase.id}`;
   416	        const logPath = `${p.dir}/children/${childId}.log`;
   417	        ensureDir(io.fs, `${p.dir}/children`);
   418	        const childSpec = buildChildArgv(assignment, prompt, { root });
   419	        const result = await runner(childSpec, {
   420	          timeoutMs: spec.budgets.perRoleTimeoutMin * 60_000,
   421	          graceMs: 10_000,
   422	          logPath,
   423	          maxLogBytes: 1_000_000,
   424	          platform: assignment.platform,
   425	          // Persist the child's process group the INSTANT it spawns — a
   426	          // supervisor crash must leave every in-flight child reap-able by
   427	          // the reclaiming run (G4).
   428	          onStart: (/** @type {{pid: number, pgid: number}} */ info) => {
   429	            appendEntry(io.fs, `${p.dir}/children.ndjson`, { childId, pid: info.pid, pgid: info.pgid, startedAt: io.now() });
   430	          },
   431	        });
   432	        // Retire the child the moment it resolves — a reclaim must never kill
   433	        // a completed child's (possibly OS-recycled) process group (H3).
   434	        appendEntry(io.fs, `${p.dir}/children.ndjson`, { childId, endedAt: io.now() });
   435	
   436	        // Classification reads the child's LOG (the frozen transcript), so a
   437	        // limit banner routes to failover even when a verdict parsed.
   438	        const transcript = io.fs.existsSync(logPath) ? io.fs.readFileSync(logPath, 'utf8') : '';
   439	        const cls = classify({ text: transcript, exitCode: result.exitCode ?? 0, platform: assignment.platform, table }).class;
   440	
   441	        if (cls === 'usage-limit' || cls === 'model-unavailable' || cls === 'other-error' || cls === 'throttle' || cls === 'auth') {
   442	          failoverAttempt += 1;
   443	          const decision = await runFailover({
   444	            root,
   445	            io,
   446	            config,
   447	            role: phase.role,
   448	            assignment,
   449	            transcript,
   450	            exitCode: result.exitCode ?? 1,
   451	            sessionHint,
   452	            probes: null,
   453	            avoid,
   454	            avoidEntries,
   455	            attempt: failoverAttempt,
   456	            loopState: { runId, phaseIndex: state.phaseIndex, iterations: state.iterations, status: state.status },
   457	          });
   458	          if (decision.action === 'park') {
   459	            await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.PARK, reason: decision.reason });
   460	            io.stderr.write(`baton loop run: parked — ${decision.reason}${'resumeAt' in decision && decision.resumeAt ? ` (resume around ${decision.resumeAt})` : ''}\n`);
   461	            return EXIT_PARKED;
   462	          }
   463	          if (decision.action === 'relaunch') {
   464	            // Platform avoidance is PER-DEATH, not cumulative: runFailover
   465	            // already avoided the just-died platform when it resolved the
   466	            // relaunch. Accumulating here would exhaust every platform on a
   467	            // chained A→B→A failover and park instead of returning to A
   468	            // (acceptance constraint 2). Entry-level avoidance (dead MODELS)
   469	            // does accumulate — a rejected model stays rejected.
   470	            if (Array.isArray(decision.avoidEntries)) avoidEntries = decision.avoidEntries;
   471	            forcedAssignment = { ...decision.assignment, role: phase.role };
   472	            forcedPrompt = typeof decision.prompt === 'string' && decision.prompt.length > 0 ? decision.prompt : null;
   473	            continue; // relaunch the same phase on the new assignment
   474	          }
   475	          continue; // retry: re-spawn the same phase once more
   476	        }
   477	
   478	        const verdict = String(result.verdict ?? 'BLOCKED');
   479	        if (verdict === 'APPROVED' || verdict === 'APPROVED_WITH_NOTES') {
   480	          await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.PHASE_ADVANCE });
   481	          phaseDone = true;
   482	
   483	          // The smoke gate fires right after the smoke-build phase completes.
   484	          if (phase.id === 'smoke-build' && typeof spec.smoke?.cmd === 'string' && spec.smoke.cmd.length > 0) {
   485	            const [cmd, ...cmdArgs] = spec.smoke.cmd.split(/\s+/);
   486	            let smokeOut = '';
   487	            try {
   488	              const r = await io.execFile(cmd, cmdArgs, { cwd: root });
   489	              smokeOut = `${r?.stdout ?? ''}${r?.stderr ?? ''}`;
   490	            } catch (err) {
   491	              smokeOut = `smoke command failed: ${/** @type {any} */ (err)?.message ?? String(err)}`;
   492	            }
   493	            await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.SMOKE_AWAIT });
   494	            const git = await gitSnapshot({ execFile: io.execFile, cwd: root, fs: io.fs });
   495	            const inputs = {
   496	              stateDigest: dedupeKey(io.fs.readFileSync(p.state, 'utf8')),
   497	              smokeCmd: spec.smoke.cmd,
   498	              smokeOutputDigest: dedupeKey(smokeOut),
   499	              gitHead: gitHeadOf(git),
   500	              gitContentDigest: gitContentOf(git),

     1	/**
     2	 * Loop run state — snapshot + journal + replay for the Layer-2 supervisor,
     3	 * mirroring the bundle store's crash-tolerance idioms (atomic tmp+rename
     4	 * snapshots, torn-tail-tolerant NDJSON, seq-dedup replay). Everything lives
     5	 * under <root>/.handoff/loop/; the supervisor is the sole writer. Plan:
     6	 * docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md §"baton loop",
     7	 * §"Smoke gate", §"Supervisor lifetime and recovery", §"Caps".
     8	 */
     9	import { appendEntry, readAllTolerant } from '../util/jsonl.mjs';
    10	import { atomicWriteJson, safeReadJson, ensureDir } from '../util/fsx.mjs';
    11	import { dedupeKey } from '../util/ids.mjs';
    12	
    13	export const LOOP_STATE_SCHEMA = 'baton/loop-state@1';
    14	
    15	/** The frozen status vocabulary. */
    16	export const LOOP_STATUS = Object.freeze({
    17	  RUNNING: 'running',
    18	  AWAITING_SMOKE_APPROVAL: 'awaiting-smoke-approval',
    19	  PARKED: 'parked',
    20	  ESCALATED: 'escalated',
    21	  DONE: 'done',
    22	});
    23	
    24	/** The frozen journal event vocabulary. */
    25	export const LOOP_EVENT = Object.freeze({
    26	  PHASE_ADVANCE: 'phase-advance',
    27	  GATE_ITERATION: 'gate-iteration',
    28	  PARK: 'park',
    29	  RESUME: 'resume',
    30	  SMOKE_AWAIT: 'smoke-await',
    31	  SMOKE_APPROVE: 'smoke-approve',
    32	});
    33	
    34	// Every gate loops at most 5 times — a repo-wide hard invariant (AGENTS.md
    35	// §Review gates); the reducer refuses a 6th and escalates instead.
    36	const GATE_ITERATION_CAP = 5;
    37	
    38	/** @param {string} root */
    39	export function loopPaths(root) {
    40	  const dir = `${root}/.handoff/loop`;
    41	  return { dir, state: `${dir}/state.json`, journal: `${dir}/journal.ndjson` };
    42	}
    43	
    44	/**
    45	 * Fresh run state — pure and deterministic from the injected io (identity
    46	 * facts + clock only; no Date.now/Math.random, which would break replay).
    47	 * @param {any} spec a VALIDATED loop spec @param {any} io
    48	 */
    49	export function initLoopState(spec, io) {
    50	  const runId = `loop-${dedupeKey({ host: io.host, pid: io.pid, startTime: io.startTime, at: io.now() }).slice(0, 12)}`;
    51	  /** @type {Record<string, number>} */
    52	  const iterations = {};
    53	  for (const p of spec.phases ?? []) {
    54	    if (/gate/.test(String(p.id))) iterations[p.id] = 0;
    55	  }
    56	  return {
    57	    schema: LOOP_STATE_SCHEMA,
    58	    runId,
    59	    goal: spec.goal,
    60	    phaseCount: Array.isArray(spec.phases) ? spec.phases.length : 0,
    61	    phaseIndex: 0,
    62	    iterations,
    63	    status: LOOP_STATUS.RUNNING,
    64	    parkReason: null,
    65	    escalation: null,
    66	    smokeApproval: null,
    67	    createdAt: io.now(),
    68	    journalSeq: 0,
    69	  };
    70	}
    71	
    72	/**
    73	 * The pure transition reducer. Never mutates its input; unknown events and
    74	 * refused transitions return the input state unchanged (a new reference is
    75	 * only produced for a real transition).
    76	 * @param {any} state @param {any} ev
    77	 */
    78	export function applyLoopEvent(state, ev) {
    79	  switch (ev?.type) {
    80	    case LOOP_EVENT.PHASE_ADVANCE: {
    81	      const next = state.phaseIndex + 1;
    82	      if (next >= state.phaseCount) return { ...state, phaseIndex: state.phaseCount, status: LOOP_STATUS.DONE };
    83	      return { ...state, phaseIndex: next };
    84	    }
    85	    case LOOP_EVENT.GATE_ITERATION: {
    86	      const gate = String(ev.gate);
    87	      const current = state.iterations?.[gate] ?? 0;
    88	      // The 6th attempt is REFUSED: the counter stays at the cap and the run
    89	      // escalates with a record naming the gate — never a 6th run.
    90	      if (current >= GATE_ITERATION_CAP) {
    91	        return { ...state, status: LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED, escalation: { gate, iteration: current } };
    92	      }
    93	      return { ...state, iterations: { ...state.iterations, [gate]: current + 1 } };
    94	    }
    95	    case LOOP_EVENT.PARK:
    96	      return { ...state, status: LOOP_STATUS.PARKED, parkReason: ev.reason ?? null };
    97	    case LOOP_EVENT.RESUME:
    98	      // Only a PARK is resumable; an escalation is an operator decision.
    99	      if (state.status !== LOOP_STATUS.PARKED) return state;
   100	      return { ...state, status: LOOP_STATUS.RUNNING, parkReason: null };
   101	    case LOOP_EVENT.SMOKE_AWAIT:
   102	      return { ...state, status: LOOP_STATUS.AWAITING_SMOKE_APPROVAL };
   103	    case LOOP_EVENT.SMOKE_APPROVE: {
   104	      // Guarded transition: only meaningful while awaiting, and only with the
   105	      // caller's verifySmokeToken result stamped verified === true. A missing
   106	      // or false `verified` is unverified — it never resumes the run.
   107	      if (state.status !== LOOP_STATUS.AWAITING_SMOKE_APPROVAL) return state;
   108	      if (ev.verified !== true) return state;
   109	      return { ...state, status: LOOP_STATUS.RUNNING, smokeApproval: { token: ev.token, at: ev.ts ?? null } };
   110	    }
   111	    default:
   112	      return state;
   113	  }
   114	}
   115	
   116	/**
   117	 * Atomic snapshot write (tmp + rename under .handoff/loop).
   118	 * @param {string} root @param {any} state @param {any} io
   119	 */
   120	export async function writeLoopState(root, state, io) {
   121	  const p = loopPaths(root);
   122	  ensureDir(io.fs, p.dir);
   123	  atomicWriteJson(io.fs, p.state, state);
   124	}
   125	
   126	/**
   127	 * Append one journal event; allocates the next seq past
   128	 * max(snapshot.journalSeq, journal tail) unless the entry carries one.
   129	 * @param {string} root @param {any} ev @param {any} io
   130	 * @returns {Promise<number>} the persisted seq
   131	 */
   132	export async function appendLoopEvent(root, ev, io) {
   133	  const p = loopPaths(root);
   134	  ensureDir(io.fs, p.dir);
   135	  let seq = ev.seq;
   136	  if (typeof seq !== 'number') {
   137	    const snap = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.state);
   138	    const snapSeq = snap.ok && typeof snap.value?.journalSeq === 'number' ? snap.value.journalSeq : 0;
   139	    let tailSeq = 0;
   140	    if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
   141	      for (const e of readAllTolerant(io.fs, p.journal).entries) {
   142	        if (typeof e.seq === 'number' && e.seq > tailSeq) tailSeq = e.seq;
   143	      }
   144	    }
   145	    seq = Math.max(snapSeq, tailSeq) + 1;
   146	  }
   147	  const ts = typeof ev.ts === 'string' ? ev.ts : io.now();
   148	  const entry = {
   149	    ...ev,
   150	    seq,
   151	    ts,
   152	    dedupeKey: typeof ev.dedupeKey === 'string' && ev.dedupeKey.length > 0 ? ev.dedupeKey : dedupeKey({ seq, ts, type: ev.type, gate: ev.gate ?? null }),
   153	  };
   154	  appendEntry(io.fs, p.journal, entry);
   155	  return seq;
   156	}
   157	
   158	/**
   159	 * Load = snapshot + torn-tail-tolerant replay of journal events PAST the
   160	 * snapshot's seq. Duplicate seqs/dedupeKeys apply exactly once, so a
   161	 * supervisor-death recovery never re-runs a completed iteration.
   162	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io
   163	 * @returns {Promise<{state: any, warnings: string[]}>}
   164	 */
   165	export async function loadLoopState(root, io) {
   166	  const p = loopPaths(root);
   167	  /** @type {string[]} */
   168	  const warnings = [];
   169	  const snap = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.state);
   170	  if (!snap.ok) return { state: null, warnings: ['no loop state snapshot found'] };
   171	  let state = snap.value;
   172	  const baseSeq = typeof state.journalSeq === 'number' ? state.journalSeq : 0;
   173	
   174	  if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
   175	    const { entries, warnings: jw } = readAllTolerant(io.fs, p.journal);
   176	    for (const w of jw ?? []) warnings.push(`journal: ${w.kind ?? 'warning'} at line ${w.line ?? '?'}`);
   177	    const applied = new Set();
   178	    let maxSeq = baseSeq;
   179	    for (const e of entries) {
   180	      const seq = typeof e.seq === 'number' ? e.seq : null;
   181	      if (seq !== null && seq <= baseSeq) continue; // already folded into the snapshot
   182	      const key = e.dedupeKey ?? (seq !== null ? `seq:${seq}` : null);
   183	      if (key !== null && applied.has(key)) continue; // replayed duplicate — apply once
   184	      if (seq !== null && applied.has(`seq:${seq}`)) continue;
   185	      state = applyLoopEvent(state, e);
   186	      if (key !== null) applied.add(key);
   187	      if (seq !== null) {
   188	        applied.add(`seq:${seq}`);
   189	        if (seq > maxSeq) maxSeq = seq;
   190	      }
   191	    }
   192	    state = { ...state, journalSeq: maxSeq };
   193	  }
   194	  return { state, warnings };
   195	}
   196	
   197	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   198	// Smoke-approval token — a STRUCTURED digest over the six bound inputs, so a
   199	// stale approval is refused NAMING what moved (mirrors the receive receipt
   200	// token's driftedInputs discipline).
   201	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   202	
   203	const SMOKE_TOKEN_PREFIX = 'smk1';
   204	const SMOKE_TOKEN_FIELDS = /** @type {const} */ ([
   205	  'stateDigest',
   206	  'smokeCmd',
   207	  'smokeOutputDigest',
   208	  'gitHead',
   209	  'gitContentDigest',
   210	  'childAssignment',
   211	]);
   212	
   213	/**
   214	 * Deterministic structured token. Per-field digests keep field boundaries
   215	 * (no concatenation collisions) and let verification name drifted inputs.
   216	 * Missing bound fields throw — a token over partial inputs is meaningless.
   217	 * @param {Record<string, string>} inputs
   218	 * @returns {string}
   219	 */
   220	export function smokeApprovalToken(inputs) {
   221	  for (const f of SMOKE_TOKEN_FIELDS) {
   222	    if (typeof inputs?.[f] !== 'string') throw new Error(`smoke approval token: required input '${f}' is missing`);
   223	  }
   224	  return `${SMOKE_TOKEN_PREFIX}.` + SMOKE_TOKEN_FIELDS.map((f) => dedupeKey(inputs[f]).slice(0, 10)).join('.');
   225	}
   226	
   227	/**
   228	 * Recompute against current inputs; positional comparison names every
   229	 * drifted input. Never throws — partial current inputs simply cannot verify.
   230	 * @param {string} presented @param {Record<string, string>} inputs
   231	 * @returns {{ok: true} | {ok: false, driftedInputs: string[]}}
   232	 */
   233	export function verifySmokeToken(presented, inputs) {
   234	  /** @type {string} */
   235	  let current;
   236	  try {
   237	    current = smokeApprovalToken(inputs);
   238	  } catch {
   239	    return { ok: false, driftedInputs: SMOKE_TOKEN_FIELDS.filter((f) => typeof inputs?.[f] !== 'string') };
   240	  }
   241	  if (presented === current) return { ok: true };
   242	  const a = String(presented ?? '').split('.');
   243	  const b = current.split('.');
   244	  /** @type {string[]} */
   245	  const drifted = [];
   246	  SMOKE_TOKEN_FIELDS.forEach((f, i) => {
   247	    if (a[i + 1] !== b[i + 1]) drifted.push(f);
   248	  });
   249	  return { ok: false, driftedInputs: drifted.length > 0 ? drifted : [...SMOKE_TOKEN_FIELDS] };
   250	}

     1	/**
     2	 * Guarded worktree transaction layer (Layer 3) — every git operation is a

     4	 * around each child, merges under a supervisor merge lock gated on a

     7	 * docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md §"Worktree transaction

     9	 */
    10	import { ensureIgnoreLine } from '../scaffold/gitignore.mjs';
    11	import { atomicWriteJson, ensureDir } from '../util/fsx.mjs';
    12	
    13	export const WORKTREE_BRANCH_RE = /^baton\/wt-(a|b)\//;
    14	
    15	const SEATS = /** @type {const} */ (['a', 'b']);
    16	const SOLE_AUTHOR = { name: 'SakshamUboweja', email: 'ssakshamu@gmail.com' };
    17	
    18	// Mirrors the doctor git-guard trailer discipline (core/src/commands/doctor.mjs).
    19	const TRAILER_PATTERNS = [
    20	  /co-authored-by:[^\n]*\b(claude|gpt|copilot|codex)\b/i,
    21	  /generated (with|by)[^\n]*\bclaude\b/i,
    22	  /\u{1F916}/u,
    23	];
    24	
    25	// One declared log format — per-commit fields NUL-delimited in this exact
    26	// order (sha, author name/email, committer name/email, body), records
    27	// RS-terminated. The parser below consumes exactly this shape.
    28	const LOG_FORMAT = '%H%x00%an%x00%ae%x00%cn%x00%ce%x00%B%x1e';
    29	
    30	/** @param {string} root */
    31	export function worktreePaths(root) {
    32	  const dir = `${root}/.worktrees`;
    33	  /** @type {Record<string, string>} */
    34	  const seats = { a: `${dir}/wt-a`, b: `${dir}/wt-b` };
    35	  return { dir, seats };
    36	}
    37	
    38	/** @param {string} seat */
    39	const seatBranch = (seat) => `baton/wt-${seat}/base`;
    40	
    41	/** @param {any} io @param {string} cwd @param {string[]} args */
    42	async function git(io, cwd, args) {
    43	  return io.execFile('git', args, { cwd });
    44	}
    45	
    46	/** Parse `git worktree list --porcelain` into [{path, branch}]. @param {string} out */
    47	function parseWorktreeList(out) {
    48	  /** @type {Array<{path: string, branch: string | null}>} */
    49	  const entries = [];
    50	  for (const block of String(out).split(/\n\n+/)) {
    51	    const path = block.match(/^worktree (.+)$/m)?.[1];
    52	    if (!path) continue;
    53	    const branch = block.match(/^branch refs\/heads\/(.+)$/m)?.[1] ?? null;
    54	    entries.push({ path, branch });
    55	  }
    56	  return entries;
    57	}
    58	
    59	/**
    60	 * Ensure both seat worktrees exist on their namespaced branches and that
    61	 * `.worktrees/` is gitignored. Idempotent: an existing seat is left alone.
    62	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io
    63	 * @returns {Promise<{ok: boolean, worktrees: Array<{seat: string, path: string, branch: string}>, gitignoreChanged: boolean}>}
    64	 */
    65	export async function setupWorktrees(root, io) {
    66	  const p = worktreePaths(root);
    67	
    68	  const gitignorePath = `${root}/.gitignore`;
    69	  const existing = io.fs.existsSync(gitignorePath) ? io.fs.readFileSync(gitignorePath, 'utf8') : '';
    70	  const ensured = ensureIgnoreLine(existing, '.worktrees/');
    71	  if (ensured.changed) io.fs.writeFileSync(gitignorePath, ensured.text);
    72	
    73	  const listed = parseWorktreeList((await git(io, root, ['worktree', 'list', '--porcelain'])).stdout);
    74	  /** @type {Array<{seat: string, path: string, branch: string}>} */
    75	  const worktrees = [];
    76	  for (const seat of SEATS) {
    77	    const path = p.seats[seat];
    78	    const present = listed.find((e) => e.path === path);
    79	    if (present) {
    80	      worktrees.push({ seat, path, branch: present.branch ?? seatBranch(seat) });
    81	      continue;
    82	    }
    83	    const branch = seatBranch(seat);
    84	    await git(io, root, ['worktree', 'add', '-b', branch, path]);
    85	    worktrees.push({ seat, path, branch });
    86	  }
    87	  return { ok: true, worktrees, gitignoreChanged: ensured.changed };
    88	}
    89	
    90	/**
    91	 * Preflight before a child runs in a seat: the worktree is listed, its index
    92	 * is clean, and HEAD sits on the expected branch. Refusals name the failed
    93	 * check and never issue destructive git.
    94	 * @param {string} root @param {{seat: string, branch: string}} target @param {any} io
    95	 * @returns {Promise<{ok: true} | {ok: false, check: string, refusal: string}>}
    96	 */
    97	export async function preflightWorktree(root, target, io) {
    98	  const path = worktreePaths(root).seats[target.seat];
    99	  const listed = parseWorktreeList((await git(io, root, ['worktree', 'list', '--porcelain'])).stdout);
   100	  if (!listed.some((e) => e.path === path)) {
   101	    return { ok: false, check: 'missing-worktree', refusal: `worktree ${path} is not listed by git — run setup or self-heal first` };
   102	  }
   103	  const status = (await git(io, path, ['status', '--porcelain'])).stdout.trim();
   104	  if (status.length > 0) {
   105	    return { ok: false, check: 'dirty-index', refusal: `worktree ${path} has a dirty index/working tree — a clean seat is required before a child runs` };
   106	  }
   107	  const head = (await git(io, path, ['rev-parse', '--abbrev-ref', 'HEAD'])).stdout.trim();
   108	  if (head !== target.branch) {
   109	    return { ok: false, check: 'wrong-branch', refusal: `worktree ${path} HEAD is on '${head}', expected branch '${target.branch}'` };
   110	  }
   111	  return { ok: true };
   112	}
   113	
   114	/**
   115	 * Postflight after a child: HEAD still on the expected branch (checked in the
   116	 * seat cwd) and main unmoved from the pre-child capture.
   117	 * @param {string} root @param {{seat: string, branch: string, mainSha: string}} target @param {any} io
   118	 * @returns {Promise<{ok: true} | {ok: false, check: string, refusal: string}>}
   119	 */
   120	export async function postflightWorktree(root, target, io) {
   121	  const path = worktreePaths(root).seats[target.seat];
   122	  const head = (await git(io, path, ['rev-parse', '--abbrev-ref', 'HEAD'])).stdout.trim();
   123	  if (head !== target.branch) {
   124	    return { ok: false, check: 'head-drift', refusal: `the child left HEAD on '${head}' instead of the expected branch '${target.branch}'` };
   125	  }
   126	  const main = (await git(io, root, ['rev-parse', 'main'])).stdout.trim();
   127	  if (main !== target.mainSha) {
   128	    return { ok: false, check: 'main-moved', refusal: `main moved under the child (was ${target.mainSha}, now ${main}) — only the merger writes main` };
   129	  }
   130	  return { ok: true };
   131	}
   132	
   133	/**
   134	 * Full-range attribution scan over main..branch — the merge gate. Every
   135	 * commit must be authored AND committed by the sole author, with no forbidden
   136	 * AI-attribution trailer in the body. The range is never windowed.
   137	 * @param {string} root @param {string} branch @param {any} io
   138	 * @returns {Promise<{ok: true} | {ok: false, offending: string, reason: string}>}
   139	 */
   140	export async function attributionScan(root, branch, io) {
   141	  const out = (await git(io, root, ['log', `main..${branch}`, `--format=${LOG_FORMAT}`])).stdout;
   142	  for (const record of String(out).split('\x1e')) {
   143	    if (record.trim().length === 0) continue;
   144	    const [sha, an, ae, cn, ce, body = ''] = record.replace(/^\n/, '').split('\x00');
   145	    if (an !== SOLE_AUTHOR.name || ae !== SOLE_AUTHOR.email) {
   146	      return { ok: false, offending: sha, reason: `commit ${sha} has a foreign author identity (${an} <${ae}>) — sole-author is required` };
   147	    }
   148	    if (cn !== SOLE_AUTHOR.name || ce !== SOLE_AUTHOR.email) {
   149	      return { ok: false, offending: sha, reason: `commit ${sha} has a foreign committer identity (${cn} <${ce}>) — sole-author is required` };
   150	    }
   151	    if (TRAILER_PATTERNS.some((re) => re.test(body))) {
   152	      return { ok: false, offending: sha, reason: `commit ${sha} carries a forbidden AI-attribution trailer (co-authored/generated-with/emoji) in its message` };
   153	    }
   154	  }
   155	  return { ok: true };
   156	}
   157	
   158	const mergeLockPath = (/** @type {string} */ root) => `${root}/.handoff/loop/merge.lock`;
   159	
   160	/**
   161	 * Merge an approved subtask branch into main — one guarded transaction:
   162	 * acquire the merge lock (a held lock refuses), gate on the attribution scan,
   163	 * merge at the repo root, abort-and-park on conflict, then ff-only sync both
   164	 * seats. The lock is released on EVERY exit path after acquisition.
   165	 * @param {string} root @param {{branch: string}} input @param {any} io
   166	 * @returns {Promise<{ok: true, branch: string} | {ok: false, reason: string}>}
   167	 */
   168	export async function mergeSubtask(root, input, io) {
   169	  const lock = mergeLockPath(root);
   170	  if (io.fs.existsSync(lock)) {
   171	    return { ok: false, reason: `the merge lock at ${lock} is held by another merger — refusing a concurrent merge` };
   172	  }
   173	  ensureDir(io.fs, `${root}/.handoff/loop`);
   174	  atomicWriteJson(io.fs, lock, { host: io.host, pid: io.pid, at: io.now() });
   175	  try {
   176	    const scan = await attributionScan(root, input.branch, io);
   177	    if (scan.ok !== true) return { ok: false, reason: scan.reason };
   178	
   179	    try {
   180	      await git(io, root, ['merge', input.branch]);
   181	    } catch (err) {
   182	      // Conflict (or any merge failure): abort, never auto-resolve — park.
   183	      try {
   184	        await git(io, root, ['merge', '--abort']);
   185	      } catch {
   186	        // Nothing to abort — the failure predates a merge state.
   187	      }
   188	      const msg = /** @type {any} */ (err)?.stdout || /** @type {any} */ (err)?.message || String(err);
   189	      return { ok: false, reason: `merge conflict — aborted and parked for the operator: ${String(msg).split('\n')[0]}` };
   190	    }
   191	
   192	    const seats = worktreePaths(root).seats;
   193	    for (const seat of SEATS) {
   194	      try {
   195	        await git(io, seats[seat], ['merge', '--ff-only', 'main']);
   196	      } catch (err) {
   197	        const msg = /** @type {any} */ (err)?.stderr || /** @type {any} */ (err)?.message || String(err);
   198	        return { ok: false, reason: `worktree wt-${seat} could not fast-forward to main — corruption signal, parked: ${String(msg).split('\n')[0]}` };
   199	      }
   200	    }
   201	    return { ok: true, branch: input.branch };
   202	  } finally {
   203	    try {
   204	      if (io.fs.existsSync(lock)) io.fs.unlinkSync(lock);
   205	    } catch {
   206	      // Best effort — a leaked lock is visible and operator-recoverable.
   207	    }
   208	  }
   209	}
   210	
   211	/**
   212	 * Self-heal a raw-deleted seat: git still lists it but the directory is gone
   213	 * — prune the stale metadata and re-add on the same branch.
   214	 * @param {string} root @param {{seat: string, branch: string}} target @param {any} io
   215	 * @returns {Promise<{ok: boolean, healed: boolean}>}
   216	 */
   217	export async function selfHealWorktree(root, target, io) {
   218	  const path = worktreePaths(root).seats[target.seat];
   219	  const listed = parseWorktreeList((await git(io, root, ['worktree', 'list', '--porcelain'])).stdout);
   220	  const stale = listed.some((e) => e.path === path) && !io.fs.existsSync(path);
   221	  if (!stale) return { ok: true, healed: false };
   222	  await git(io, root, ['worktree', 'prune']);
   223	  await git(io, root, ['worktree', 'add', path, target.branch]);
   224	  return { ok: true, healed: true };
   225	}
   226	
   227	/**
   228	 * Tear down BOTH seats: remove the worktrees and delete their namespaced
   229	 * branches. Anything outside .worktrees/wt-* or baton/wt-* is never touched,
   230	 * so a teardown leaves the repo a plain git repo (acceptance constraint 5).
   231	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io
   232	 * @returns {Promise<{ok: boolean, removed: string[]}>}
   233	 */
   234	export async function teardownWorktrees(root, io) {
   235	  const p = worktreePaths(root);
   236	  const listed = parseWorktreeList((await git(io, root, ['worktree', 'list', '--porcelain'])).stdout);
   237	  /** @type {string[]} */
   238	  const removed = [];
   239	  for (const seat of SEATS) {
   240	    const entry = listed.find((e) => e.path === p.seats[seat]);
   241	    if (!entry) continue;
   242	    await git(io, root, ['worktree', 'remove', '--force', p.seats[seat]]);
   243	    // Delete the seat's CURRENT branch and its setup base branch — a seat
   244	    // that moved on to subtask branches orphans its base otherwise
   245	    // (acceptance constraint 5: teardown leaves a plain git repo). Only
   246	    // namespaced branches are ever deleted.
   247	    /** @type {Set<string>} */
   248	    const toDelete = new Set();
   249	    if (entry.branch && WORKTREE_BRANCH_RE.test(entry.branch)) toDelete.add(entry.branch);
   250	    toDelete.add(seatBranch(seat));
   251	    for (const branch of toDelete) {
   252	      if (!WORKTREE_BRANCH_RE.test(branch)) continue;
   253	      try {
   254	        await git(io, root, ['branch', '-D', branch]);
   255	      } catch {
   256	        // Already gone (current === base, or deleted earlier) — fine.
   257	      }
   258	    }
   259	    removed.push(seat);
   260	  }

   806	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   807	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   808	    const killCalls = [];
   809	    io.processKill = (/** @type {number} */ pid, /** @type {any} */ sig) => { killCalls.push({ pid, sig }); return true; };
   810	    // The resumed run's in-flight child (pgid 9002) is alive; run 1's retired X is not.
   811	    io.processAlive = (/** @type {number} */ p) => p === 9002;
   812	    io.fs.mkdirSync(DIR, { recursive: true });
   813	    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${DIR}/supervisor.lock`, JSON.stringify({ host: 'loop-host', pid: 999999, startTime: 7, runId: 'crashed' }));
   814	    io.fs.writeFileSync(
   815	      `${DIR}/children.ndjson`,
   816	      // Run 1: childId '001-plan' started (pgid 9001) then retired.
   817	      JSON.stringify({ childId: '001-plan', pid: 8001, pgid: 9001, startedAt: T0 }) + '\n' +
   818	        JSON.stringify({ childId: '001-plan', endedAt: T0 }) + '\n' +
   819	        // Run 2 (the crashed resume): the SAME childId '001-plan' started again (pgid 9002), never retired.
   820	        JSON.stringify({ childId: '001-plan', pid: 8002, pgid: 9002, startedAt: T0 }) + '\n',
   821	    );
   822	
   823	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   824	    assert.equal(code, 0, `the reclaiming run completes; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
   825	    assert.ok(killCalls.some((k) => Math.abs(k.pid) === 9002), `the resumed in-flight X (9002) is killed despite an earlier retire of the same childId; kills: ${JSON.stringify(killCalls)}`);
   826	  });
   827	
   828	  it('RED (I2b): a NORMAL (non-reclaim) acquisition truncates prior-invocation records — a stale retire cannot linger to mask a future child', async () => {
   829	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
   830	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   831	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   832	    // A prior invocation's stale records (a distinctive pgid 4242000) are present;
   833	    // no lock, so this is a clean acquisition.
   834	    io.fs.mkdirSync(DIR, { recursive: true });
   835	    io.fs.writeFileSync(
   836	      `${DIR}/children.ndjson`,
   837	      JSON.stringify({ childId: '001-plan', pid: 4242000, pgid: 4242000, startedAt: T0 }) + '\n' + JSON.stringify({ childId: '001-plan', endedAt: T0 }) + '\n',
   838	    );
   839	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   840	    assert.equal(code, 0);
   841	    const raw = io.files()[`${DIR}/children.ndjson`] ?? '';
   842	    assert.doesNotMatch(raw, /4242000/, "a prior invocation's records are truncated at acquisition — they cannot mask a current child");
   843	  });
   844	});

   867	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 0, 'no child spawned on an unstamped-state refusal');

   869	  });

   872	// ===========================================================================
   873	// I5 (B5) — the run lock passes startTime to processAlive: a recycled pid
   874	// (alive by pid alone but failing the pid+startTime pair) is reclaimed, not
   875	// refused as live.
   876	describe('loop run — lock reclaim uses pid+startTime (I5)', () => {
   877	  const DIR = `${loopPaths('/repo').dir}`;
   878	  it('RED (I5): a lock whose pid is alive by a 1-arg check but fails the (pid, startTime) pair is treated as DEAD and reclaimed', async () => {
   879	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
   880	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   881	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   882	    // pid 7777 reads as alive for ANY defined startTime EXCEPT the recorded 999
   883	    // (which is the reused-pid case → dead). A 1-arg check, or a wrong-but-defined
   884	    // second arg, would read live and wedge the lock. Only passing the RECORDED
   885	    // startTime (999) reclaims — so the fake is inverted vs. a "match one value"
   886	    // trap, and the calls are recorded to prove the pair is passed through.
   887	    const aliveCalls = [];
   888	    io.processAlive = (/** @type {number} */ pid, /** @type {number | undefined} */ startTime) => {
   889	      aliveCalls.push({ pid, startTime });
   890	      if (pid !== 7777) return false;
   891	      return startTime !== 999; // dead ONLY for the recorded (pid, startTime) pair
   892	    };
   893	    io.fs.mkdirSync(DIR, { recursive: true });
   894	    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${DIR}/supervisor.lock`, JSON.stringify({ host: 'loop-host', pid: 7777, startTime: 999, runId: 'recycled' }));
   895	
   896	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   897	    assert.equal(code, 0, `a reused-pid lock is reclaimed (not refused as live); stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
   898	    assert.ok(io.__runner.calls.length >= 1, 'the run proceeds after reclaiming the reused-pid lock');
   899	    assert.ok(
   900	      aliveCalls.some((c) => c.pid === 7777 && c.startTime === 999),
   901	      `the reclaim passes the RECORDED (pid, startTime) pair to processAlive; calls: ${JSON.stringify(aliveCalls)}`,
   902	    );
   903	  });
   904	});

   912	// ===========================================================================
   913	// I1 (A1/B1) — auto-advance requires a supervisor-owned MERGE RECEIPT, not just
   914	// is-ancestor: a stale empty branch at main's tip is is-ancestor-true too, and
   915	// must PARK, not silently skip the subtask.
   916	//
   917	// Merge-receipt shape (I1 design): append-only `.handoff/loop/merges.ndjson`,
   918	// one line per merged subtask `{ subtaskId, branch, mergedAt }`. Auto-advance on
   919	// resume requires is-ancestor(branch, main) AND a receipt for that subtask.
   920	describe('pipeline — I1: merge-receipt-gated auto-advance', () => {
   921	  const MERGES = `${loopPaths('/repo').dir}/merges.ndjson`;
   922	  const seedReceipt = (io, subtaskId, branch) => {
   923	    io.fs.mkdirSync(loopPaths('/repo').dir, { recursive: true });
   924	    io.fs.writeFileSync(MERGES, JSON.stringify({ subtaskId, branch, mergedAt: T0 }) + '\n');
   925	  };
   926	  const receipts = (io) => {
   927	    const raw = io.files()[MERGES];
   928	    return typeof raw === 'string' ? raw.split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim() !== '').map((l) => JSON.parse(l)) : [];
   929	  };
   930	
   931	  it('RED (I1b): a receipt-backed already-merged subtask ADVANCES on resume (no writer, no empty-branch park)', async () => {
   932	    const subtasks = [{ id: 't1', title: 'first' }, { id: 't2', title: 'second' }];
   933	    // t1 is genuinely merged: is-ancestor true, empty main..t1, AND a receipt.
   934	    const git = pipelineGit({ mergedBranches: ['baton/wt-a/subtask-t1'] });
   935	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks }), git, runner: undefined });
   936	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask()); // enough for t2 only
   937	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   938	    seedPipelineState(io, { flavor: 'pipeline', specDigest: dedupeKey(subtasks), phaseIndex: 0, phaseCount: 2 });
   939	    seedReceipt(io, 't1', 'baton/wt-a/subtask-t1');
   940	
   941	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   942	    assert.equal(code, 0, `a receipt-backed merged subtask advances and the run completes; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
   943	    assert.doesNotMatch(io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText(), /EMPTY branch|nothing to review or merge|stale/i, 'a receipt-backed subtask is not mistaken for empty/stale');
   944	    const writers = writersOf(io.__runner);
   945	    assert.ok(writers.length >= 1, 'the unmerged subtask still runs a writer');
   946	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(writers[0]), '-C'), WT_B, 'the first writer is t2 (wt-b) — t1 was recognized as merged (receipt) and skipped');
   947	  });
   948	
   949	  it('RED (I1a): a stale EMPTY branch at main tip (is-ancestor true, NO receipt) PARKS as stale — never auto-advances', async () => {
   950	    const subtasks = [{ id: 't1', title: 'first' }, { id: 't2', title: 'second' }];
   951	    // t1's branch is empty at main's tip: is-ancestor true, empty main..t1 — but
   952	    // NO merge receipt (the writer crashed before its first commit).
   953	    const git = pipelineGit({ mergedBranches: ['baton/wt-a/subtask-t1'] });
   954	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks }), git, runner: undefined });
   955	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [...cleanSubtask(), ...cleanSubtask()]);
   956	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   957	    seedPipelineState(io, { flavor: 'pipeline', specDigest: dedupeKey(subtasks), phaseIndex: 0, phaseCount: 2 });
   958	    // No receipt seeded.
   959	
   960	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   961	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'a receipt-less is-ancestor branch parks as stale rather than silently skipping the subtask');
   962	    assert.match(io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText(), /stale/i, 'the park names the stale branch');
   963	    assert.equal(io.__git.matching(/^merge baton\//).length, 0, 'nothing is merged for a stale empty branch');
   964	  });
   965	
   966	  it('GUARD (I1 conjunction): a receipt WITHOUT is-ancestor does NOT auto-advance — the subtask is re-run, never silently skipped', async () => {
   967	    const subtasks = [{ id: 't1', title: 'first' }, { id: 't2', title: 'second' }];
   968	    // A receipt exists for t1, but the branch is NOT an ancestor of main (the
   969	    // merge did not actually land — e.g. main was rewound). A receipt-ONLY
   970	    // advance would skip t1; the required conjunction re-runs it.
   971	    const git = pipelineGit({ mergedBranches: [] }); // is-ancestor FALSE for every branch
   972	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks }), git, runner: undefined });
   973	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [...cleanSubtask(), ...cleanSubtask()]);
   974	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   975	    seedPipelineState(io, { flavor: 'pipeline', specDigest: dedupeKey(subtasks), phaseIndex: 0, phaseCount: 2 });
   976	    seedReceipt(io, 't1', 'baton/wt-a/subtask-t1'); // receipt present, ancestor absent
   977	
   978	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   979	    assert.equal(code, 0, `the run completes by re-running t1; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
   980	    const writers = writersOf(io.__runner);
   981	    assert.ok(writers.length >= 1, 'a writer ran');
   982	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(writers[0]), '-C'), WT_A, 'the FIRST writer is t1 (wt-a) — a receipt alone did NOT skip it (is-ancestor is a required conjunct)');
   983	  });
   984	
   985	  it('RED (I1c): a clean merge PERSISTS a durable receipt {subtaskId, branch, mergedAt} BEFORE the phase-advance is journaled', async () => {
   986	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), runner: undefined });
   987	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
   988	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   989	
   990	    // Ordering hook: at each phase-advance journal write, capture whether the t1
   991	    // receipt is ALREADY on disk (durable before the advance, not merely by EOR).
   992	    const journalPath = `${loopPaths('/repo').dir}/journal.ndjson`;
   993	    const receiptAtAdvance = [];
   994	    const realAppend = io.fs.appendFileSync.bind(io.fs);
   995	    io.fs.appendFileSync = (/** @type {any} */ path, /** @type {any} */ data) => {
   996	      if (String(path) === journalPath && /"type":"phase-advance"/.test(String(data))) {
   997	        receiptAtAdvance.push(receipts(io).some((r) => r.subtaskId === 't1'));
   998	      }
   999	      return realAppend(path, data);
  1000	    };
  1001	
  1002	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1003	    assert.equal(code, 0);
  1004	    const rec = receipts(io).find((r) => r.subtaskId === 't1');
  1005	    assert.ok(rec, 'a merge receipt is persisted after the subtask merges');
  1006	    assert.equal(rec.branch, 'baton/wt-a/subtask-t1', 'the receipt names the merged branch');
  1007	    assert.ok(typeof rec.mergedAt === 'string' && rec.mergedAt.length > 0, 'the receipt records mergedAt');
  1008	    assert.ok(receiptAtAdvance.length >= 1 && receiptAtAdvance.every(Boolean), 'the receipt is durable on disk BEFORE the phase advance is journaled (crash-safe)');
  1009	  });
  1010	});
