    86	function ownedBundle(originPlatform = 'claude-code', status = 'open') {
    87	  return {
    88	    schema: 'baton/bundle@1',
    89	    bundleId: 'b_pipe00000000000',
    90	    generation: 1,
    91	    createdAt: T0,
    92	    updatedAt: T0,
    93	    origin: { platform: originPlatform, model: 'm', sessionHint: 'loop-sup', unstable: false },
    94	    task: { goal: 'Ship the pipeline', constraints: [], acceptance: [] },
    95	    plan: { steps: [] },
    96	    decisions: [],
    97	    files: { touched: [] },
    98	    roles: { assignments: {} },
    99	    git: null,
   100	    handoff:
   101	      status === 'sealed'
   102	        ? { status: 'sealed', reason: 'seal', reasonClass: 'usage-limit', toPlatformHint: null, finalizedAt: T0, receive_log: [] }
   103	        : { status: 'open', reason: null, reasonClass: null, toPlatformHint: null, finalizedAt: null, receive_log: [] },
   104	    journalSeq: 0,
   105	    compaction: { droppedDecisions: 0, note: null },
   106	    dedupeRing: [],
   107	  };
   108	}
   109	
   110	/**
   111	 * A STRICT, stateful fake git: unstubbed commands reject; worktree add/list and
   112	 * checkout -b are modeled so setup + preflight/postflight + merge cohere.
   113	 * `over` overrides specific responses (e.g. a merge conflict).
   114	 */
   115	function pipelineGit({ over = {}, log = CLEAN_LOG, existingBranches = [], dirtyCwds = [], mergedBranches = [] } = {}) {
   116	  const added = /** @type {Map<string,string>} */ (new Map()); // path -> branch
   117	  const branchAt = /** @type {Map<string,string>} */ (new Map()); // cwd -> current branch
   118	  const branches = new Set(existingBranches); // stale/pre-existing namespaced branches
   119	  const dirty = new Set(dirtyCwds); // seat cwds whose index is dirty
   120	  const deleted = new Set(); // seats listed by git but raw-deleted on disk
   121	  const merged = new Set(mergedBranches); // branches already merged into main (ancestor; empty main..branch)
   122	  const calls = /** @type {any[]} */ ([]);
   123	  const lockProbeRef = { fn: /** @type {null | (() => boolean)} */ (null) };
   124	  let mainSha = MAIN_SHA; // mutable so a child can "move main" mid-subtask
   125	
   126	  const list = () => {
   127	    const entries = [{ path: '/repo', branch: 'main' }];
   128	    for (const [path, branch] of added) entries.push({ path, branch: branchAt.get(path) ?? branch });
   129	    return entries.map((e) => `worktree ${e.path}\nHEAD ${MAIN_SHA}\nbranch refs/heads/${e.branch}\n`).join('\n');
   130	  };
   131	
   132	  const fn = (/** @type {string} */ cmd, /** @type {string[]} */ args = [], /** @type {any} */ opts = {}) => {
   133	    const argstr = args.join(' ');
   134	    calls.push({ cmd, args, argstr, cwd: opts?.cwd, lockPresent: lockProbeRef.fn ? lockProbeRef.fn() : undefined });
   135	    if (cmd !== 'git') return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error('spawn ENOENT'), { code: 'ENOENT' }));
   136	
   137	    // Explicit overrides win.
   138	    const okey = Object.keys(over).sort((a, b) => b.length - a.length).find((k) => argstr.includes(k));
   139	    if (okey) {
   140	      const r = over[okey];
   141	      if (r?.reject) return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error(r.stderr || 'git failed'), { code: r.code ?? 1, stdout: r.stdout ?? '', stderr: r.stderr ?? '' }));
   142	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: r?.stdout ?? '', stderr: r?.stderr ?? '' });
   143	    }
   144	
   145	    // A raw-deleted seat: git still LISTS it, but any command IN that cwd fails
   146	    // (the directory is gone). Only self-heal (prune + re-add) recovers it.
   147	    if (opts?.cwd && deleted.has(opts.cwd) && !argstr.startsWith('worktree ')) {
   148	      return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error(`fatal: cannot chdir to '${opts.cwd}': No such file or directory`), { code: 128, stderr: 'No such file or directory\n' }));
   149	    }
   150	    if (argstr.startsWith('worktree list')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: list(), stderr: '' });
   151	    if (argstr.startsWith('worktree add')) {
   152	      const path = args.find((a) => a.startsWith('/repo/.worktrees/'));
   153	      const bi = args.indexOf('-b');
   154	      const branch = bi >= 0 ? args[bi + 1] : 'baton/wt-x/base';
   155	      if (path) { added.set(path, branch); branchAt.set(path, branch); deleted.delete(path); }
   156	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
   157	    }
   158	    if (argstr.startsWith('worktree prune')) { deleted.clear(); return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' }); }
   159	    if (argstr.startsWith('worktree remove')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
   160	    if (argstr.startsWith('checkout -b')) {
   161	      const target = args[args.indexOf('-b') + 1];
   162	      if (branches.has(target)) return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error(`fatal: a branch named '${target}' already exists`), { code: 128, stderr: `fatal: a branch named '${target}' already exists\n` }));
   163	      branches.add(target);
   164	      if (opts?.cwd) branchAt.set(opts.cwd, target);
   165	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
   166	    }
   167	    if (argstr.startsWith('status --porcelain')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: dirty.has(opts?.cwd) ? ' M dirty.txt\n' : '', stderr: '' });
   168	    if (argstr === 'rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD') return Promise.resolve({ stdout: `${branchAt.get(opts?.cwd) ?? 'main'}\n`, stderr: '' });
   169	    if (argstr.startsWith('rev-parse')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: `${mainSha}\n`, stderr: '' });
   170	    // merge-base --is-ancestor <branch> main : exit 0 iff <branch> is already in main.
   171	    if (argstr.startsWith('merge-base --is-ancestor')) {
   172	      const b = args[args.indexOf('--is-ancestor') + 1];
   173	      return merged.has(b) ? Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' }) : Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error('not an ancestor'), { code: 1, stdout: '', stderr: '' }));
   174	    }
   175	    if (argstr.startsWith('log ')) {
   176	      // A merged branch has NO commits ahead of main (empty main..<branch>).
   177	      const range = args.find((a) => /\.\./.test(String(a))) ?? '';
   178	      const branch = String(range).split('..')[1];
   179	      if (branch && merged.has(branch)) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
   180	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: log, stderr: '' });
   181	    }
   182	    if (argstr.startsWith('merge')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: 'Fast-forward', stderr: '' });
   183	    if (argstr.startsWith('branch -D')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
   184	    return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error(`unstubbed git: ${argstr}`), { code: 'ENOSTUB' }));
   185	  };
   186	  fn.calls = calls;
   187	  fn.git = () => calls.filter((c) => c.cmd === 'git');
   188	  fn.issued = (/** @type {RegExp} */ re) => calls.some((c) => c.cmd === 'git' && re.test(c.argstr));
   189	  fn.matching = (/** @type {RegExp} */ re) => calls.filter((c) => c.cmd === 'git' && re.test(c.argstr));
   190	  fn.withCwd = (/** @type {RegExp} */ re, /** @type {string} */ cwd) => calls.filter((c) => c.cmd === 'git' && re.test(c.argstr) && c.cwd === cwd);
   191	  fn.setLockProbe = (/** @type {() => boolean} */ f) => { lockProbeRef.fn = f; };
   192	  fn.moveMain = (/** @type {string} */ sha) => { mainSha = sha; }; // a child "moves main" mid-subtask
   193	  fn.setBranch = (/** @type {string} */ cwd, /** @type {string} */ branch) => { branchAt.set(cwd, branch); };
   194	  // Explicit seed: git LISTS this worktree; `raw` marks its dir as deleted on
   195	  // disk (any in-cwd command fails until prune + re-add self-heals it).
   196	  fn.seedWorktree = (/** @type {string} */ path, /** @type {string} */ branch, /** @type {{raw?: boolean}} */ o = {}) => {
   197	    added.set(path, branch);
   198	    branchAt.set(path, branch);
   199	    if (o.raw) deleted.add(path);
   200	  };
   201	  return fn;
   202	}
   203	
   204	function fakeRunner(io, script) {
   205	  const queue = [...script];
   206	  const calls = /** @type {any[]} */ ([]);
   207	  const fn = (/** @type {any} */ spec, /** @type {any} */ opts = {}) => {
   208	    calls.push({ spec, opts, command: spec?.command, args: spec?.args ?? [], env: spec?.env });
   209	    const r = queue.shift() ?? { verdict: 'APPROVED' };
   210	    if (typeof r.logContent === 'string' && opts.logPath) {
   211	      const dir = opts.logPath.slice(0, opts.logPath.lastIndexOf('/'));
   212	      io.fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
   213	      io.fs.writeFileSync(opts.logPath, r.logContent);
   214	    }
   215	    // A child may perturb the world (e.g. move main) between spawn and postflight.
   216	    if (typeof r.effect === 'function') r.effect();
   217	    return Promise.resolve({ timedOut: false, exitCode: r.exitCode ?? 0, verdict: r.verdict ?? 'APPROVED', findings: r.findings ?? '', logPath: opts.logPath });
   218	  };
   219	  fn.calls = calls;
   220	  return fn;

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	// Fake `io` object matching the injected-io contract from docs/design/core.md:
     2	// { cwd, env, stdin, stdout, stderr, fs, execFile, now }. Backed by memfs.
     3	// stdout/stderr collect writes; io.stdoutText()/io.stderrText() flatten them.
     4	// now() returns a settable ISO string (io.setNow). execFile resolves from an
     5	// execResults map keyed by the joined command string.
     6	//
     7	// Wave-B2 additions (bundle lock layer) — all optional, defaulted, and purely
     8	// additive so no prior consumer is affected:
     9	//   host / pid / startTime  — the process-identity facts the lock model writes
    10	//     into owner.json and compares on contention (see plan §Concurrency, Lock
    11	//     model iterations 3+4).
    12	//   processAlive(pid, startTime) — the "provably dead" oracle. Default: only
    13	//     THIS io's own {pid, startTime} is alive; every other descriptor reads as
    14	//     dead. Override per test to model a live foreign owner or a pid/start-time
    15	//     mismatch (reused pid).
    16	//   newFencingToken() — the injected per-acquisition token source. Deterministic
    17	//     monotonic counter ("tok-1", "tok-2", …), matching the injected-io
    18	//     discipline already used for now()/execFile so lock acquisition is
    19	//     reproducible in tests rather than drawing from Math.random.
    20	
    21	import { makeMemfs } from './memfs.mjs';
    22	
    23	export function makeIo({
    24	  files = {},
    25	  env = {},
    26	  stdin = '',
    27	  execResults = {},
    28	  now = '2026-07-11T00:00:00.000Z',
    29	  host = 'host-A',
    30	  pid = 4242,
    31	  startTime = 111000,
    32	  processAlive = null,
    33	  fencingSeed = 1,
    34	  execPath = process.execPath,
    35	} = {}) {
    36	  const { fs, files: filesSnapshot } = makeMemfs(files);
    37	  const outChunks = [];
    38	  const errChunks = [];
    39	  let currentNow = now;
    40	  let tokenCounter = fencingSeed - 1;
    41	  const defaultAlive = (p, s) => p === pid && s === startTime;
    42	
    43	  const stdout = { write: (s) => { outChunks.push(String(s)); return true; } };
    44	  const stderr = { write: (s) => { errChunks.push(String(s)); return true; } };
    45	
    46	  const execFile = (cmd, args = [], /* opts */ _opts = {}) => {
    47	    const key = [cmd, ...(Array.isArray(args) ? args : [])].join(' ');
    48	    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    49	      if (!(key in execResults)) {
    50	        const e = new Error(`spawn ${cmd} ENOENT`);
    51	        e.code = 'ENOENT';
    52	        return reject(e);
    53	      }
    54	      const r = execResults[key];
    55	      const code = r.code ?? 0;
    56	      const out = r.stdout ?? '';
    57	      const err = r.stderr ?? '';
    58	      if (code !== 0) {
    59	        // util.promisify(execFile) rejects on nonzero exit; error carries code + streams.
    60	        const e = new Error(`Command failed: ${key}`);
    61	        e.code = code;
    62	        e.stdout = out;
    63	        e.stderr = err;
    64	        return reject(e);
    65	      }
    66	      resolve({ stdout: out, stderr: err });
    67	    });
    68	  };
    69	
    70	  return {
    71	    cwd: '/repo',
    72	    env,
    73	    stdin,
    74	    stdout,
    75	    stderr,
    76	    fs,
    77	    execFile,
    78	    now: () => currentNow,
    79	    setNow: (iso) => { currentNow = iso; },
    80	    execPath,
    81	    host,
    82	    pid,
    83	    startTime,
    84	    processAlive: processAlive || defaultAlive,
    85	    // Tiny retry budget: unit tests exercising live-lock refusal stay fast
    86	    // while the retry code path itself still runs (production default: 120x25ms).
    87	    lockRetry: { attempts: 2, delayMs: 2 },
    88	    newFencingToken: () => `tok-${++tokenCounter}`,
    89	    stdoutText: () => outChunks.join(''),
    90	    stderrText: () => errChunks.join(''),
    91	    files: filesSnapshot,
    92	  };
    93	}

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	/**
     2	 * Append-only JSONL journal IO, tolerant of the corruption modes a
     3	 * kill-mid-write can produce: a torn (unterminated) tail, a corrupt middle
     4	 * line, and out-of-order sequence numbers.
     5	 */
     6	
     7	/**
     8	 * Append one entry as a single JSON line.
     9	 * @param {any} fs @param {string} path @param {unknown} obj
    10	 */
    11	export function appendEntry(fs, path, obj) {
    12	  fs.appendFileSync(path, JSON.stringify(obj) + '\n');
    13	}
    14	
    15	/**
    16	 * Read every parseable entry. Never throws.
    17	 * Warnings: {line, kind: 'torn-tail'|'parse-error'|'seq-order', ...}.
    18	 * A non-monotonic numeric seq is flagged but the entry is kept — replay and
    19	 * audit must still see it.
    20	 * @param {any} fs @param {string} path
    21	 * @returns {{entries: any[], warnings: any[]}}
    22	 */
    23	export function readAllTolerant(fs, path) {
    24	  /** @type {any[]} */
    25	  const entries = [];
    26	  /** @type {any[]} */
    27	  const warnings = [];
    28	
    29	  let raw;
    30	  try {
    31	    raw = fs.readFileSync(path, 'utf8');
    32	  } catch {
    33	    return { entries, warnings };
    34	  }
    35	  if (raw === '') return { entries, warnings };
    36	
    37	  const terminated = raw.endsWith('\n');
    38	  const lines = raw.split('\n');
    39	  if (terminated) lines.pop();
    40	
    41	  let prevSeq = null;
    42	  for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i += 1) {
    43	    const lineNo = i + 1;
    44	    const isLast = i === lines.length - 1;
    45	    const torn = isLast && !terminated;
    46	    let parsed;
    47	    try {
    48	      parsed = JSON.parse(lines[i]);
    49	    } catch {
    50	      warnings.push(torn ? { line: lineNo, kind: 'torn-tail' } : { line: lineNo, kind: 'parse-error' });
    51	      continue;
    52	    }
    53	    if (typeof parsed?.seq === 'number') {
    54	      if (prevSeq !== null && parsed.seq <= prevSeq) {
    55	        warnings.push({ line: lineNo, kind: 'seq-order', prev: prevSeq, seq: parsed.seq });
    56	      }
    57	      prevSeq = prevSeq === null ? parsed.seq : Math.max(prevSeq, parsed.seq);
    58	    }
    59	    entries.push(parsed);
    60	  }
    61	  return { entries, warnings };
    62	}

    42	  supervisor death leaves live children, and lock reclaim neither kills nor
    43	  adopts them.
    44	  Fix: journal child start {pid, pgid} before spawn; on dead-lock reclaim
    45	  kill recorded groups (provably-dead discipline), then continue or park.

    60	  the park/escalation messages instruct resuming.
    61	  Fix: `baton loop resume` (PARKED only; escalation stays operator-only);
    62	  correct the messages.
    63

    65	  phase with smoke.cmd set silently skips the human gate.
    66	  Fix: validateLoopSpec rejects smoke.cmd without a smoke-build phase.

    91	# Iteration 2 (post-fold f7960e3) — both reviewers BLOCKED again
    92	
    93	Reviewer-a: 2 blocking. Reviewer-b: 1 blocking, 3 major, 4 minor.
    94	Deduplicated:
    95	
    96	## Blocking
    97	- **H1 (B1)** — the pipeline WRITER prompt omits the verdict-tail contract
    98	  while the flow requires an APPROVED writer verdict: every real writer
    99	  parses BLOCKED-unparseable and escalates at the cap. Fix: verdict-tail
   100	  block in the writer prompt + a prompt-contract pin.
   101	- **H2 (A1 + B2)** — pipeline failover is unbounded: avoid/avoidEntries
   102	  always [], decision.avoidEntries discarded, and no total child budget —
   103	  dead-model ping-pong loops forever. Fix: per-subtask avoidEntries carry +
   104	  a child-budget guard that parks on exhaustion; always-limit runner pin.
   105	- **H3 (A2 + B5)** — children.ndjson is append-only: completed children are
   106	  never retired, so a dead-lock reclaim kills any live (possibly
   107	  OS-recycled) recorded pgid. Fix: completion records retiring entries when
   108	  a child resolves; reclaim kills only still-active records.
   109	
   110	## Major
   111	- **H4 (B3)** — core/bin/baton.mjs never defines io.processKill, so a real
   112	  reclaim skips every kill and then DELETES children.ndjson. Fix:
   113	  process.kill-backed processKill in the bin io; only clear the registry
   114	  after a kill pass with a real killer.
   115	- **H5 (B4)** — loop and pipeline share state.json with no flavor/spec
   116	  binding: cross-flavor resume skips subtasks or reports a false done. Fix:
   117	  stamp {flavor, specDigest} at init; refuse/archive-and-reinit on mismatch.
   118	
   119	## Minor (folded where cheap)
   120	- H6 (B6): ESCALATION.md wording still says "resume with baton loop run".
   121	- H7 (B7): a crash between merge and PHASE_ADVANCE resumes into a
   122	  misleading empty-branch park — an already-merged branch should advance.
   123	- H8 (B8b): the lock runId (`sup-<pid>`) is uncorrelated with the state
   124	  runId — cosmetic.

   126	# Iteration 3 (post-fold 64e6cb5) — both reviewers BLOCKED
   127	
   128	Reviewer-a: 1 blocking, 1 major, 1 minor. Reviewer-b: 2 major, 3 minor.
   129	All eight H-dispositions audited GENUINE by both. Deduplicated (dual-raised
   130	findings take the higher severity):
   131	
   132	## Blocking
   133	- **I1 (A1 + B1)** — the H7 already-merged recognizer false-positives on a
   134	  stale EMPTY branch: `merge-base --is-ancestor` succeeds for a branch
   135	  created by `checkout -b` whose writer never committed (its tip IS an old
   136	  main HEAD), so a crash anywhere in the writer's pre-first-commit window
   137	  makes the resume silently skip the subtask and report it done. Fix:
   138	  auto-advance only on a supervisor-owned merge receipt — journal/persist a
   139	  per-subtask merged record immediately after mergeSubtask succeeds and
   140	  require receipt + is-ancestor; anything else parks as a stale branch. Pin
   141	  the negative: a pre-existing empty branch at main's tip must NOT advance.
   142	
   143	## Major
   144	- **I2 (B2)** — retire records mask in-flight children across invocations:
   145	  childSeq resets per run and children.ndjson survives normal acquisitions,
   146	  so a resumed run reuses childIds a prior invocation already retired —
   147	  reclaim's childId-keyed retired set then SKIPS killing the crashed resume
   148	  run's live child (the G4 orphan escape returns in resume-then-crash). Fix:
   149	  truncate children.ndjson on every successful lock acquisition (clean prior
   150	  exit leaves only retired records; a dead owner's records were just
   151	  processed by the reclaim pass). Pin: retire in run 1, same childId
   152	  in-flight in run 2, dead lock → reclaim MUST kill.
   153	- **I3 (A2 + B4)** — unstamped state bypasses the H5 binding: the
   154	  typeof-string guards accept state.json with no flavor/specDigest, so
   155	  pre-stamp (or stripped) state resumes under either command with exactly
   156	  the misalignment H5 prevents. Fix: missing stamp on an EXISTING state =
   157	  mismatch — refuse exit 2 with the archive instruction (no legacy
   158	  population worth grandfathering); reconcile older recovery fixtures that
   159	  seed unstamped states.
   160	
   161	## Minor
   162	- **I4 (A3 + B3)** — the loop-side H5 refusals return after
   163	  acquireSupervisorLock but before the releasing try/finally, leaking
   164	  supervisor.lock (pipeline's equivalents are correctly inside its finally).
   165	  Fix: run the flavor/specDigest checks inside the released region (or
   166	  before acquiring).
   167	- **I5 (B5)** — acquireSupervisorLock calls io.processAlive(other.pid)
   168	  without the recorded startTime, so a recycled pid reads as a live
   169	  supervisor and wedges the lock until hand-deleted. Fix: pass
   170	  other.startTime so pid-reuse verification engages.
   171	
   172	## Disposition plan
   173	Fold all five now (I4/I5 are one-line-cheap; I1–I3 sit on the crash/recovery
   174	acceptance constraint). TDD flow: test-author pins → verifier → implement →
   175	Gate-2 iteration 4 (cap 5, both reviewers at 3).
